1. TEN CLEAN-SLATE ARCHITECTURAL INVARIANTS (P1-P10)
------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[P1] Clean-Room Originality (Principles Only, Never Copies)
     Transfer mathematical, biophysical, neurological, and ecological principles from peer-reviewed biology and A-Life literature, while engineering 100% original parametric morphologies, body silhouettes, connectomes, shaders, and source code. Zero copied meshes, sprites, weight dumps, or repositories.
[P2] Compensated FP64 First-Law Closure & Second-Law Sink
     Conserve mass and enthalpy across all 11 pools (`E_patch..E_heat_phys + E_diss_num`) in IEEE `FP64` with Kahan-Neumaier compensation (`|ΔE| / (E_0 + E_in,cum) < 1e-12`; never `Q16.16`). Enforce Second-Law irreversibility (`0` heat-to-ATP backflow) and book XPBD numerical damping explicitly (`ΔE_diss_num ≥ -1e-15`).
[P3] Causal Wiring & Zero Cosmetic Parts (`C-NC`)
     Every declared organ, neural sub-circuit, soft hydrostatic chamber, neuromodulator, and transducer must causally alter internal state and downstream behavior within its active ecological regime. Display-only variables, dead matrices, and scripted animations fail conformance.
[P4] Seed Firewall, Domain-Isolated PRNG & Two-Tier Reproducibility
     Separate `Practice (100–999)`, `Tuning (2000–2999)`, and `Locked Held-Out Test (≥5000, burn-on-fail)` seeds. Key `Philox4x32-10` counters by `(ver, seed, domain, entity_id, organ_id, tick, purpose, draw_idx)` with `WORLD_EXOG` isolated from `ENTITY_INT`. Tier-1 CPU/WASM uses core-shipped `exp_det`/`log_det` and `.alifebin` snapshots for bit-exact SHA-256 hashes; Tier-2 GPU/surrogate bulk mode verifies preregistered observable distribution parity.
[P5] Soft Embodied Allostasis, Chemoton Chemistry & Real Mortality
     Ground survival in deformable XPBD hydrostatic boundaries (`|ΔV|/V_0 < 1%`), Gánti's 3-cycle Chemoton (`A`, membrane `T`, template polymer `pV_n`), Michaelis-Menten saturation, 3-phase fasting, clotting/regeneration, and irreversible death returning carcass mass to dome detritus.
[P6] Controlled Multi-Arm Causal & FWER Statistical Proof
     Evaluate claims across `1. Intact`, `2. Passive Soft-Body (0-Brain)`, `3. Shuffled Stream`, `4. Targeted Lesion`, plus `Size-Matched Sham Lesion` (for ablations) and `Competent Simple Baseline` (for complex substrates). Use power-sized paired locked seeds (`N ≥ 24` large effects; `N = 64–120` moderate effects), exact paired Wilcoxon/sign-flip tests with **Holm-Bonferroni FWER (`α ≤ 0.05`)**, within-pair dominance `δ_pair ≥ 0.33`, and one-sided non-inferiority (`LCB_95% > -Δ_NI`) on prior floors.
[P7] Neutral Operational Framing & Static/Mutant Anti-LARP Guard
     Treat `Butlin-14`, `F1–F10`, and forked-snapshot `PCI*` strictly as operational computational-dynamics indicators—never metaphysical claims of subjective sentience. Enforce AST/symbol-import oracle bans and reject all 5 negative mutants (`Leaky-2e-11, Cosmetic, Oracle, Frozen, Shuffled`).
[P8] Multicellular Ontogeny, Metamorphosis & Open-Ended Biosphere
     Grow bodies and brains from yolk-funded founder cells via GRN cleavage, differential adhesion, apoptosis, and size-proportional juvenile sleep pruning; preserve learned aversions across holometabolous metamorphosis inside an `O(N_pop + C_pairs)` closed trophic dome.
[P9] Strict Observer Independence & Bounded Telemetry
     In Tier-1 canonical runs, camera pose, open UI tabs, exports, and the Frame-Time Governor adjust **render & lossy UI decimation ONLY**—never physics meshes, ray counts, or `WSE` timing (`UI-open hash == headless hash`). Scope zero-copy strictly to Sim-Worker ↔ UI-Worker `SharedArrayBuffer` (`SeqLock` 3-retry bound + double-buffer fallback).
[P10] Hardware Frugality, Explicit Arena Accounting & Multirate Clocks
     Use flat `SoA` `TypedArray` arenas (`0` post-warmup heap allocations across `10,000` ticks), tiled sparse `CSR` / 32-synapse packed `int4`/`b1.58` blocks with separate plasticity/queue budgets, the `5-Gear Deterministic Multirate Scheduler`, `0 ≤ Q ≤ 10` qubit caps, and contiguous hysteresis Governor bands.

2. UNIFIED 11-POOL COMPENSATED FP64 THERMODYNAMIC CONTRACT
------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Representation : IEEE-754 FP64 with Kahan-Neumaier compensated summation (or int64 1-microjoule integer quanta); NEVER Q16.16 fixed-point.
Drift Gate     : |(Sum_11(E_pools(t)) + E_export_out_cum(t)) - (E_0 + E_solar_in_cum(t) + E_import_in_cum(t))| / (E_0 + E_solar_in_cum(t)) < 1e-12
Second Law     : Heat sinks (E_heat_phys, E_diss_num) are strictly one-way irreversible sinks (dE_heat_phys/dt ≥ 0, zero back-conversion to E_atp, E_stores, or mechanical work; ΔE_diss_num < -1e-15 triggers anti-free-energy fault).
  Pool 01 | E_patch        | Primary producer / substrate chemical enthalpy standing stock (kJ)
  Pool 02 | E_gut          | Unabsorbed chyme enthalpy inside organism digestive lumen (kJ)
  Pool 03 | E_blood        | Circulating glucose, free fatty acids, amino acids & beta-HB ketones (kJ)
  Pool 04 | E_stores       | Hepatic glycogen + adipose triacylglycerol reserve pools (kJ)
  Pool 05 | E_atp          | Immediate high-energy phosphate currency gating spikes, pumps & motors (kJ)
  Pool 06 | E_tissue       | Structural somatic & neural biomass enthalpy (kJ)
  Pool 07 | E_kinetic      | Macroscopic translational & rotational soft-body kinetic energy (kJ)
  Pool 08 | E_elastic      | Reversible XPBD viscoelastic membrane & tendon strain potential energy (kJ)
  Pool 09 | E_detritus     | Excreted urea/feces + post-mortem carcass labile organic pools (kJ)
  Pool 10 | E_heat_phys    | Irreversible metabolic, frictional & spike-dissipation thermal sink (kJ; monotone non-decreasing)
  Pool 11 | E_diss_num     | Explicit XPBD / implicit-integrator numerical damping sink (kJ; must satisfy ΔE_diss_num ≥ -1e-15 per step)

3. DISAMBIGUATED COMPLEXITY SYMBOL LEGEND
------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  N_units            : Neural units / compartments per creature brain (reference Tier-W focal = 50,000; Tier-L laptop = 8,192).
  E_syn              : Directed stored synaptic edges per creature brain (reference focal = 10^8 at density ρ = 4.0%; Tier-L = 500,000).
  k                  : Active spiking fraction per Gear-1 (5 ms) step; biological sparse regime k ≈ 0.01–0.04 (2–8 Hz mean firing rate).
  N_pop              : Active organisms in dome/biosphere; broad-phase spatial hash is O(N_pop + C_pairs), where C_pairs = local candidate contacts.
  V_mesh             : Soft-body viscoelastic mesh vertices per organism (fixed per experiment in Tier-1 canonical runs: e.g. 24, 32, or 64).
  R_rays             : Active sensory rays / plume sample probes per organism step; B_bands = cochlear / lateral-line spectral filter bands.
  D_lat              : Latent world-model, working-memory, or VSA hypervector dimension (typically 128–4,096).
  M_pat / M_mod      : M_pat = stored Modern Hopfield episodic patterns; M_mod = parallel specialist modules competing for workspace ignition.
  G_sp / G_plan      : G_sp = spline intervals per KAN edge; G_plan = nodes in internal Riemannian planning graph.
  K_iter             : Bounded inner solver iterations per step (e.g., XPBD constraint sweeps K=4..8, implicit chemistry Newton steps K=2..4).
  Q                  : Coherent qubits in optional internal quantum register; integer-clamped 0 ≤ Q ≤ 10 (statevector = 2^(Q+4) bytes ≤ 16 KiB).

6. 36-ITEM FORENSIC TRIAGE REGISTRY
------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  [PRB-01] (◆ PROB @ L2 Soft Body) Rigid-Sphere Embodiment Gap
    Hazard: Modeling organisms as rigid circles/spheres eliminates compliant gripping, narrow-gap squeezing, peristalsis, and passive terrain damping.
    Fix   : Build 2D/3D XPBD viscoelastic membrane + `|ΔV|/V_0 < 1%` hydrostatic coelom (`SOFT01–SOFT10`).
  [PRB-02] (◆ PROB @ L1 Chemoton) Missing Wet Autopoietic Core
    Hazard: Single scalar `energy` counters lack stoichiometric coupling between metabolism, membrane surface area, and template replication.
    Fix   : Implement Gánti Chemoton (`A`, lipid `T`, polymer `pV_n`) + Michaelis-Menten saturation (`SOFT21–SOFT25`).
  [PRB-03] (◆ PROB @ L3/L7 Agency) Missing Sensorimotor Forward Model
    Hazard: Without an internal efference-copy predictor, self-generated soft-body deformation triggers false external threat/obstacle reflexes.
    Fix   : Wire motor-to-sensory forward predictor (`AE-2`, `M073`) subtracting expected self-reafference.
  [PRB-04] (◆ PROB @ L8 Ontogeny) No Metamorphosis / Cell Sorting
    Hazard: Scalar mass growth omits embryonic cell sorting, apoptotic sculpting, and larval-to-adult body remodeling with memory survival.
    Fix   : Implement Steinberg adhesion, apoptosis, and Blackiston-assay pupal remodeling (`SOFT32–35`, `M084`).
  [PRB-05] (◆ PROB @ Sec 5.4 Eval) Missing Sham & Passive Controls
    Hazard: Comparing Intact only against uncalibrated cuts conflates passive body physics and random damage size with true circuit function.
    Fix   : Require 4 core arms + `Size-Matched Sham Lesion` + `Competent Simple Baseline` (`P6`).
  [PRB-06] (◆ PROB @ Sec 9 Arena) Unseparated Weight vs Plasticity RAM
    Hazard: Counting only packed weight bits hides eligibility queues, consolidation cascades, masks, neuron states, and GPU staging buffers.
    Fix   : Enforce full-process memory inventory separating packed weights from bounded `+0.40 B/syn` plasticity rings (`ALF-004`).
  [ISS-01] (◈ ISSU @ Web Runtime) SharedArrayBuffer Origin Isolation
    Hazard: Browsers disable `SharedArrayBuffer` unless top-level `COOP: same-origin` + `COEP: require-corp` headers establish `crossOriginIsolated`.
    Fix   : Assert `self.crossOriginIsolated === true` at boot (`HW14`); fall back cleanly to double-buffered `postMessage` transfer.
  [ISS-02] (◈ ISSU @ Host Silicon) Mobile/Laptop Thermal Throttling
    Hazard: Sustained SIMD/WebGPU bursts push mobile silicon past 90°C, dropping host clocks and desynchronizing wall-clock assumptions.
    Fix   : Decouple integer `sim_tick` from `wall_time`; drive contiguous Frame-Time Governor (`HW21`) via EMA `ms/frame`.
  [ISS-03] (◈ ISSU @ Data Export) Spreadsheet 1,048,576-Row Ceiling
    Hazard: Dumping raw spike rasters or synapse tables directly into `.xlsx`/`.xls` crashes writers or silently truncates past `1,048,576` rows.
    Fix   : Route full tensors to `Parquet`/`HDF5`; auto-shard `.xls`/`.xlsx` into `≤ 500K`-row summary sheets + manifests.
  [ISS-04] (◈ ISSU @ Governance) Multi-Artifact Specification Drift
    Hazard: Editing PDFs, CSVs, JSON, and workbooks by hand causes IDs, byte totals, and Cube cells to diverge across files.
    Fix   : Generate all 9 output formats (`PDF, JSON, MD, XML, TXT, YAML, HTML, CSV, XLS`) from one canonical v3.0 schema + audit script.
  [ISS-05] (◈ ISSU @ Data Ops) Lossy UI Ring vs Lossless Audit
    Hazard: Sharing one circular buffer between a 60 Hz UI sparkline and scientific benchmark logging drops audit events under burst load.
    Fix   : Separate overwrite-allowed UI telemetry rings (with explicit `gap_count`) from backpressure-safe lossless Export/Audit streams.
  [ISS-06] (◈ ISSU @ Repo Hygiene) Broken Git Worktree Provenance
    Hazard: Copied directories containing broken `.git` pointer files lack object stores, invalidating commit SHA provenance in test manifests.
    Fix   : Gate Stage 0 (`M003`, `GATE-01`) on `git rev-parse --verify HEAD` + clean working-tree manifest hash.
  [DIL-01] (⚖ DILM @ Physics × Evo) Continuum FEM Fidelity vs Evo Speed
    Hazard: High-element 3D continuum FEM (`~50 ms/step`) precludes multi-generation evolutionary sweeps on single workstations.
    Fix   : Ruling: Evolve on fixed-mesh XPBD hydrostats (`0.5 ms` Gear 0); validate selected genotypes in high-resolution continuum.
  [DIL-02] (⚖ DILM @ Compute × Lab) Cross-OS Bit-Exactness vs GPU Speed
    Hazard: Parallel WebGPU/CUDA float reductions are non-associative, preventing bit-identical hashes against sequential CPU code.
    Fix   : Ruling: Tier-1 CPU/WASM canonical mode enforces bit-exact hashes; Tier-2 GPU bulk mode verifies preregistered observable tolerances.
  [DIL-03] (⚖ DILM @ Evo × Testing) Open-Ended Speciation vs Fixed Floors
    Hazard: Locomotor foraging floors unfairly penalize lineages evolving into sessile filter-feeders, ambushers, or decomposers.
    Fix   : Ruling: Enforce universal First-Law + autopoietic viability globally; score behavioral competence per MAP-Elites guild cell.
  [DIL-04] (⚖ DILM @ Devo × Evo) Compact Indirect GRN vs Mutational Lethality
    Hazard: Indirect developmental GRN/NCA encodings compress genomes dramatically but risk catastrophic embryonic instability under point mutation.
    Fix   : Ruling: Canalize embryogenesis via bioelectric polarity clamping (`SOFT37`) and homeostatic `iSTDP`/synaptic scaling (`M045`).
  [DIL-05] (⚖ DILM @ Senses × HW) Per-Agent Retina/Cochlea vs Crowd Scale
    Hazard: Full 2D RGBD retinas for 500 crowd organisms saturate integrated GPU bandwidth, yet changing rays alters Tier-1 trajectory hashes.
    Fix   : Ruling: Keep sensor config fixed for all agents in Tier-1 canonical runs; enable salience-scheduled crowd LOD strictly in Tier-2.
  [DIL-06] (⚖ DILM @ Omnisensor) Live Hardware Sensors vs Repeatability
    Hazard: Live webcam/microphone/IMU inputs provide genuine open-world entropy but cannot repeat deterministically across CI runs.
    Fix   : Ruling: Explore live in Practice mode while recording immutable timestamped `.sensorlog` streams replayed bit-exactly in Test gates.
  [ERR-01] (⚠ ERR @ Telemetry Spec) 24 GB/s Naive Live Synapse Streaming
    Hazard: Streaming `10^8 × 4 B × 60 Hz = 24 GB/s` across threads violates memory-bus budgets and stalls simulation workers.
    Fix   : Corrected: Stream `O(1)` statistical sketches (`≤ 32 KB/fr`) globally; pull subgraph slices on demand (`2 Hz`) for inspected organs.
  [ERR-02] (⚠ ERR @ WSE-10 Spec) Full 825-Suite Per Online WSE Edit
    Hazard: Running full held-out benchmark suites on every millisecond in-life `WSE` plasticity edit freezes runtime execution.
    Fix   : Corrected: 3-Tier WSE Gate—Tier-0 `O(1)` induced-norm `‖W‖_2 ≤ 0.99` + ATP check online, Tier-1 sleep shadow rollout, Tier-2 promotion.
  [ERR-03] (⚠ ERR @ Scoreboard) Underpowered & Mislabeled FDR Stats
    Hazard: Conflating Holm-Bonferroni FWER with FDR `q`-values or claiming `N=16` powers 100-item medium-effect families invites false claims.
    Fix   : Corrected: Exact paired Wilcoxon/sign-flip + **Holm-Bonferroni FWER (`α ≤ 0.05`)**, power-sized `N` (`24..120`), `δ_pair`, and `LCB_95% > -Δ_NI`.
  [ERR-04] (⚠ ERR @ Sec 7.5 Cube) Z-Blind 2D Cube Compatibility
    Hazard: Scoring `Model × Precision` while ignoring `Geometry Z` misses hyperbolic (`Z4`), attractor (`Z5`), and steerable (`Z6`) low-bit failures.
    Fix   : Corrected: Full 3-axis `evaluateCubeCell(X, Y, Z)` with grayscale-safe `[OK] / [OVER] / [ADAPT] / [EXCL]` synced across PDF & CSV/XLS.
  [ERR-05] (⚠ ERR @ Lesion Method) Shared PRNG & World-Entity Coupling
    Hazard: Drawing random numbers sequentially or failing to isolate world weather from creature organs scrambles trajectories upon ablation.
    Fix   : Corrected: `Philox4x32-10` keyed by `(ver, seed, domain, entity, organ, tick, purpose, idx)` isolating `WORLD_EXOG` from `ENTITY_INT`.
  [ERR-06] (⚠ ERR @ Anti-LARP) Circular Parameter Count & Exact-Φ Claims
    Hazard: Equating parameter count with competence or claiming exact `O(2^N)` IIT `Φ` on large brains is scientifically invalid.
    Fix   : Corrected: Require forked-snapshot perturbational `PCI*` (`F2`), `C-NC` causal divergence, and Competent Simple Baselines.
  [BUG-01] (✖ BUG @ L0 Determinism) Cross-OS libm Transcendental ULP Drift
    Hazard: Host `Math.exp`/`Math.log`/`Math.tanh` implementations vary by `1–2 ULP` across OS/browser engines, breaking bit-exact hashes.
    Fix   : Ship core-owned versioned deterministic minimax/LUT `exp_det`, `log_det`, `pow_det`, and `tanh_det` in Tier-1 runtime.
  [BUG-02] (✖ BUG @ Soft Physics) Explicit Euler Soft-Spring Explosion & Free Energy
    Hazard: Explicit Euler explodes on stiff membranes, while unmonitored XPBD constraint projections can inject or dissipate unbooked energy.
    Fix   : Use XPBD (`α̃ = α/dt²`) + book positive solver damping to `E_diss_num ≥ 0` and fault on `ΔE_diss_num < -1e-15`.
  [BUG-03] (✖ BUG @ First-Law Pool) FP32 Small-Addend Absorption in Ledger
    Hazard: Adding `1e-6 kJ` spike heat to a `1e5 kJ` dome heat accumulator in `FP32` (`24` mantissa bits) rounds the increment completely to `0`.
    Fix   : Accumulate all 11 enthalpy/mass pools in `FP64` with Kahan-Neumaier compensation (`|ΔE|/(E0+Ein) < 1e-12`).
  [BUG-04] (✖ BUG @ Quantum Organ) JS 32-Bit Shift Wraparound (`1 << 32 === 1`)
    Hazard: In JS/C-style 32-bit shifts, `1 << Q` masks `Q & 31` (`Q=32` allocates 2 floats instead of guarding, while `Q=30` overflows signed int32!).
    Fix   : Validate `Number.isInteger(Q) && Q >= 0 && Q <= 10` first, then allocate `2 * (2 ** Q)` `Float64` values (`≤ 16 KiB`).
  [BUG-05] (✖ BUG @ Runtime Loop) Per-Tick Closures, BigInt Box & WASM Grow
    Hazard: Temporary closures, `BigInt` boxing in JS PRNGs, or dynamic WASM `memory.grow` inside `tick()` trigger latency spikes and view invalidation.
    Fix   : Pre-allocate fixed SoA arenas; use `Math.imul` 32-bit `Philox4x32-10`; assert `0` allocs & `0` page grows across `10,000` ticks.
  [BUG-06] (✖ BUG @ Ablation Code) Lesion Toggle Resurrecting Pruned Wires
    Hazard: Restoring a reversible lesion by setting mask bits to `1` overwrites `0`s created by developmental or sleep pruning.
    Fix   : Maintain orthogonal `structuralMask` (ontogeny/sleep) and `lesionMask` (assay), applying `effectiveMask = structuralMask & lesionMask`.
  [CNF-01] (⚡ CONF @ Drive Arbiter) Epistemic Curiosity vs Starvation Urgency
    Hazard: Additive novelty bonuses overpower homeostatic error when fuel runs low, causing starving organisms to inspect noisy traps.
    Fix   : Multiplicative Urgency Gate: weight epistemic value by sigmoid survival margin `σ((TTC - TTC_crit)/τ)`.
  [CNF-02] (⚡ CONF @ WSE / Brainstem) In-Life WSE Surgery vs Autonomic Survival
    Hazard: Self-editing operators optimizing short-horizon reward silence nociceptors, cardiac pacemakers, or metabolic accounting.
    Fix   : Hardware-enforced Protection Rings: `Ring-0` (`brainstem, nociception, ledger, harness`) uses frozen read-only views.
  [CNF-03] (⚡ CONF @ Soft Bauplan) Cuticle Armor Stiffening vs Squeeze/Speed
    Hazard: Inducible cuticle thickening shields against toxins/bites but raises bending energy, mass, and narrow-aperture failure.
    Fix   : Expose as regulated endocrine/catch-state trade-off (`SOFT04`) modulated by predator/toxin threat cues.
  [CNF-04] (⚡ CONF @ Spiking Cortex) Fast Hebbian LTP vs Recurrent Runaway
    Hazard: Positive Hebbian loops without rapid counterbalance saturate firing rates or collapse into epileptic synchrony.
    Fix   : Couple 3-factor LTP with fast inhibitory `iSTDP`, sliding BCM threshold, `‖W‖_2 ≤ 0.99` guard, and NREM `SHY`.
  [CNF-05] (⚡ CONF @ World Boundary) Exotic Field Senses vs Anti-LARP Physics
    Hazard: Simulated bioelectric or distant-sensing channels degenerate into zero-cost oracle cheats if unattenuated.
    Fix   : Mediate every exotic sense through inverse-distance noisy in-world fields with strict per-bit ATP debit and shuffle controls.
  [CNF-06] (⚡ CONF @ Coupling Bus) Concurrent Multi-Organ State Write Races
    Hazard: Sleep flip-flop, HPA cortisol, and workspace writing shared `arousal` in arbitrary order produce order-dependent oscillations.
    Fix   : Single-writer variable ownership + canonical deterministic reducer over double-buffered `prevTick -> nextTick` state.
